## Analyst notes
- Baseline was 2026-09-28T09-17Z (35-day gap). 8 `future_lastmod` anomalies are lastmods a few seconds after fetch time (CDN regeneration at fetch moment, not meaningful drift). 25 `backdated_new_url` are new business pages with lastmod 9–24 days before first_seen. 3 reappeared URLs: /business/plugins/alpaca/, /form/codex-ambassadors/, /policies/row-privacy-policy/.
- Notable edit: /policies/privacy-policy/ re-dated Sept 10, 2026 (was May 18, 2026); Sora dropped from mission blurb; ad-partner data wording changed from "may receive" to "receive".
- Notable edit: /index/learning-to-reason-with-llms/ gained intro paragraph and retirement note for o1-preview/o1-mini.
- No fetch failures (first attempt had a missing-dependency failure, discarded and rerun cleanly).
